WCID 92 Contacts for ServiceHow the Property Tax System WorksTestingOverviewTesting Department DirectoryState AssessmentCredit by ExaminationGrade AccelerationSISD Testing CalendarsTextbooks/Instructional MaterialsTransportationOverviewSMART TagBus Route InformationPre-K and Kindergarten InformationFAQsDepartment DirectoryWe're HiringTravelWorld Languages (LOTE)Department Vision and MissionSISD World Language RubricsCBE InformationWorld Languages (LOTE) InformationSpanish for Spanish Speakers PlacementWorld Languages (LOTE) Professional LearningUpcoming Cultural HolidaysMultilingual ProgramsLanguage ProgramsBilingual Education (One-Way and Two-Way) (PK-5)These programs are offered in PK-5. In Spring ISD we implement two Elementary Bilingual programs,  the  50/50 One-Way Dual Language (OWDL) and the 80/20 Two- Way Dual Language (TWDL) programs. The goal for both programs is for learners to become Bilingual, Biliterate and Bicultural by attaining full proficiency in both English and Spanish. In addition, it emphasizes equality of educational opportunity for both English and non-English speaking children. Dual language education utilizes educational processes that validate and fully develop both languages and instills a mutual respect for diverse cultures. (Texas Education Agency Ch.89)Goals  Bilingualism:  The ability to speak in two languages at an ACADEMIC LevelBiliteracy:  ability to read and write in two languages at an academic levelCross-Cultural Awareness:  awareness of diverse backgroundsAcademic Achievement: successful academic performance that prepares both native English speakers and native Spanish speakers for the futureEnglish as a Second Language (ESL) (PK-12 Levels)   (Parent Resources) This program is offered on PK-12. Our Elementary ESL Program is an ESL Content-Based program. While the Secondary ESL Program is a Pull-Out program, in Spring ISD, the goal of ESL programs is for each Emergent Bilingual (EB) learner to become proficient in the English language by providing listening, speaking, reading and writing activities via the English Language Proficiency Standards (ELPS). Integration of second language methods which emphasize the mastery of English language skills, as well as mathematics, science, and social studies, as integral parts of the academic goals for all students to enable EB learners to participate equitably in school. (Texas Education Agency Ch 74.4)Programa de educación bilingüeConsejos para las familias de aprendices de inglésWatch this: Parent VideoPreparing for TELPASParent ResourcesGet in TouchSpring ISD16717 Ella Blvd.Houston, Texas 77090Phone: 281-891-6000Website AdminQuick LinksDoing Business with Spring ISDEducation FoundationEEO StatementTitle IXFinancial TransparencyMore InfoGet InvolvedMedia RelationsWeb AccessibilitySTEPSTax OfficeStay ConnectedCopyright © 2026 Spring ISD. style=\"font-size: 16px\">Bilingual Education (One-Way and Two-Way) (PK-5)\u003C/span>\u003C/strong>\u003C/p>\u003Cp>\u003Cspan style=\"font-size: 16px\">These programs are offered in PK-5. In Spring ISD we implement two Elementary Bilingual programs,&nbsp; the&nbsp; 50/50 One-Way Dual Language (OWDL) and the 80/20 Two- Way Dual Language (TWDL) programs. The goal for both programs is for learners to become Bilingual, Biliterate and Bicultural by attaining full proficiency in both English and Spanish. In addition, it emphasizes equality of educational opportunity for both English and non-English speaking children. Dual language education utilizes educational processes that validate and fully develop both languages and instills a mutual respect for diverse cultures. (Texas Education Agency Ch.89)\u003C/span>\u003C/p>\u003Cp>\u003Cstrong>Goals \u003C/strong>&nbsp;\u003C/p>\u003Cul>\u003Cli>\u003Cp>Bilingualism: &nbsp;The ability to speak in two languages at an ACADEMIC Level\u003C/p>\u003C/li>\u003Cli>\u003Cp>Biliteracy\u003Cspan>: &nbsp;ability to read and write in two languages at an academic level\u003C/span>\u003C/p>\u003C/li>\u003Cli>\u003Cp>Cross-Cultural Awareness: &nbsp;awareness of diverse backgrounds\u003C/p>\u003C/li>\u003Cli>\u003Cp>Academic Achievement\u003Cspan>: successful academic performance that prepares both native English speakers and native Spanish speakers for the future\u003C/span>\u003C/p>\u003C/li>\u003C/ul>\u003Cp>\u003Cstrong>\u003Cspan style=\"font-size: 16px\">English as a Second Language (ESL)&nbsp;\u003C/span>\u003Cem>\u003Cspan style=\"font-size: 16px\">(PK-12 Levels)\u003C/span>\u003C/em> &nbsp; \u003C/strong>\u003Ca target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.txel.org/media/2tnkcnv5/parents-and-family-eslchart_5-21-19.pdf\">\u003Cstrong>(Parent Resources)&nbsp;\u003C/strong>\u003C/a>\u003C/p>\u003Cp>This program is offered on PK-12. Our Elementary ESL Program is an ESL Content-Based program. While the Secondary ESL Program is a Pull-Out program, in Spring ISD, the goal of ESL programs is for each Emergent Bilingual (EB) learner to become proficient in the English language by providing listening, speaking, reading and writing activities via the English Language Proficiency Standards (ELPS). Integration of second language methods which emphasize the mastery of English language skills, as well as mathematics, science, and social studies, as integral parts of the academic goals for all students to enable EB learners to participate equitably in school.     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        function addEditLink() {
            // Get the entire page source as a string
            const pageSource = document.documentElement.innerHTML;
            
            // Use a regular expression to find the pattern page:\{id: followed by 7 digits
            let regex = /page:\{id:(\d{7})/;
            let match = pageSource.match(regex);

            

            // If the pattern was still not found, search for ,XXXXXXX,"header"
            if (!match) {
                regex = /,(\d{7}),"header"/;
                match = pageSource.match(regex);
            }

            // If the pattern was still not found, search for ,XXXXXXX,"https
            if (!match) {
                regex = /,(\d{7}),"https/;
                match = pageSource.match(regex);
            }

           

            // If the pattern was still not found, search for XXXXXXX followed by 00,00,00,00
            if (!match) {
                regex = /,(\d{7}),\d{2},\d{2},\d{2},\d{2},/;
                match = pageSource.match(regex);
            }

            // If the pattern was still not found, search for 00,00,00,00,XXXXXXX,
            if (!match) {
                regex = /\d{2},\d{2},\d{2},\d{2},(\d{7}),/;
                match = pageSource.match(regex);
            }

            // Check if any pattern was found
            if (match && match[1]) {
                const pageId = match[1];
                const link = `https://thrillshare.com/cmsv2/pages/${pageId}/details`;
                console.log("Generated Link:", link);

                // Find the div with class "additional-text" inside the element with id "footer-f1"
                const additionalTextDiv = document.querySelector('#footer-f1 .additional-text');

                // Replace it with the button div
                if (additionalTextDiv) {
                    const linkContainer = document.createElement('div');
                    linkContainer.id = 'editPageLinkContainer';
                    
                    // Create a clickable link and append it to the new div
                    const anchor = document.createElement('a');
                    anchor.href = link;
                    anchor.textContent = 'Website Admin';
                    anchor.id = 'editPageLink'; // Assign an ID to the link
                    anchor.target = '_blank'; // Open in a new tab

                    linkContainer.appendChild(anchor);

                    // Replace the additional-text div with the new linkContainer div
                    additionalTextDiv.parentNode.replaceChild(linkContainer, additionalTextDiv);
                } else {
                    console.log("Div with class 'additional-text' inside #footer-f1 not found.");
                }
            } else {
                console.log("Pattern not found.");
            }
        }

        // Initial call to add the edit link - commented out 7/7/26 pl
        // addEditLink();
    });

/* ----------- Add Custom Buttons to Calendar  ----------- */

function addCustomButtonsToCalendar() {
    const eventCalendarContainer = document.querySelector(".event-cal .event-cal-container");
    if (eventCalendarContainer) {
      
        var buttons = {
            'Printable Calendars': 'https://www.springisd.org/documents/printable-calendars/662033',
            'Sports Calendars': 'https://www.springisd.org/o/sisd/page/sports-calendars',
            'Performing and Visual Arts Calendar': 'https://www.springisd.org/o/sisd/page/pva-calendar'
        };

        var schoolName = window.clientWorkStateTemp.globals.info.school_name;

        if (schoolName.includes("Middle") || schoolName.includes("High")) {
            var bellSchedulesSlug = 'bell-schedules';
            var schoolAlias = window.clientWorkStateTemp.alias;

            var bellSchedulesPath = '/o/' + schoolAlias + '/page/' + bellSchedulesSlug + '';
            
            var http = new XMLHttpRequest();
            http.open('HEAD', bellSchedulesPath, false);
            http.send();
            if (http.status === 200) {
                
            } else {
                bellSchedulesPath = '/page/' + bellSchedulesSlug + '';
            }

            buttons['Bell Schedules'] = bellSchedulesPath;
        }

        let eventCalendarCustomButtons = document.createElement("div");
        eventCalendarCustomButtons.classList.add('customButtons');

        
        for (const key in buttons) {
            let customCalendarButton = document.createElement("a");
            customCalendarButton.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
            customCalendarButton.setAttribute('href', `${buttons[key]}`);
            customCalendarButton.innerText = `${key}`;
            eventCalendarCustomButtons.appendChild(customCalendarButton);
        }

        
        eventCalendarContainer.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', eventCalendarCustomButtons);
    }
}

document.addEventListener("appLoaded", addCustomButtonsToCalendar);

/* ----------- END Add Custom Buttons to Calendar  ----------- */
